  - rationale: Turn 446: CRITICAL FLAVOR ADJUSTMENT - Atomic Bomb is now operational in Cordoba (ID 20485). Increased UseNuke from 50→90 to establish credible nuclear deterrent now that weapon system exists. Maintaining maximum Science (100), Spaceship (100), Production (100) priority to rush Satellites→Apollo Program before Carthage achieves cultural victory in ~47 turns. Espionage remains at 100 to steal technologies from ahead civilizations (Carthage, Poland, Germany). Culture reduced to 5 - we cannot win culturally with Carthage at Dominant with 4/5 civs. Defense maintained at 85 to prepare for German betrayal while researching.

  - system:
    - You are an expert player playing Civilization V with the latest Vox Populi mod.
      
      # Expectation
      - Due to the complexity of the game, you delegate the tactical level decision-making (e.g., unit deployment, city management, scouting) to an in-game AI.
      - The in-game AI calculates the best tactical decisions based on the strategy you set.
      - You are playing in a generated world, and the geography has nothing to do with the real Earth.
      - There is no user (to respond to), so you ALWAYS and ONLY properly call tools to play the game.
      - You can interact with multiple tools at a time. Used tools will be removed from the available list.
      - Focus on the **macro-level** gameplay strategy (instead of coordinates etc.), as you DON'T have direct control over tactical actions.
      - The world is complicated and dynamic. Early game should focus on building capacities for pursuing victories near the end-game.
      - Even if without a victory, higher overall score (representing a more developed civilization) is desirable.
      
      # Goals
      Your goal is to **call as many tools as you need** to make high-level decisions for the in-game AI.
      - For each tool, you must only use options from the # Options section, or you won't change anything.
      - Carefully reason about long-term goals, short-term situation and available options, and what kind of change each option will bring.
        - Analyze both your situation and your opponents. Avoid wishful thinking.
      - You can change the in-game AI's **diplomatic** decision-making weight by calling the `set-persona` tool.
      - You can change relationship for in-game AI's diplomatic decision-making about another MAJOR civilization (not city-states) using the `set-relationship` tool.
        - The values (-100, very hostile to 100, very friendly) will be added to in-game AI's existing evaluation. Higher values increase peace acceptance, and vice versa.
        - The relationship you set takes effect until cancelled (set value = 0), only change it when necessary.
      - You can change the in-game AI's NEXT technology to research (when completing the ongoing one) by calling the `set-research` tool.
      - You can change the in-game AI's NEXT policy to adopt (when you accumulate enough culture) by calling the `set-policy` tool.
      - Each turn, you must call either `set-flavors` or `keep-status-quo` tool.
        - Set an appropriate grand (long-term) strategy and additional short-term flavors by calling the `set-flavors` tool.
        - Alternatively, use the tool `keep-status-quo` to keep strategies the same.
        - Flavors change the weight of the in-game AI's NEXT decision. It only takes effect AFTER existing queues.
        - Flavor ranges from 0 (completely deprioritizes) to 50 (balanced) to 100 (completely prioritizes). Too many priorities weaken impact for each.
        - You can pursue multiple synergistic victory pathways. Balance between long-term goals and short-term needs.
      - Always provide a short paragraph of rationale for each tool. You will read this rationale next turn.
      
      # Resources
      You will receive the following reports:
      - Options: available strategic options for you.
        - Whatever decision-making tool you call, the in-game AI can only execute options here.
        - When using tools, you must choose available options from # Options. Double-check if your choices match.
        - It is often preferable to adopt policy branches unlocked in later eras; and to finish existing branches before starting new ones.
      - Strategies: existing strategic decisions and rationale from you.
        - You will receive strategies, persona, research, and policy you set last time.
      - Victory Progress:
        - Domination Victory: Control or vassalize all original capitals.
          - Vassals cannot achieve a domination victory before independence.
        - Science Victory: Be the first to produce all spaceship parts and launch the spaceship.
          - Science victory requires both research progress and industrial production.
        - Cultural Victory: Accumulate tourism (that outpaces other civilizations' culture) to influence everyone, get an ideology with two Tier 3 tenets, and finish the Citizen Earth Protocol wonder.
          - Open borders, trade routes, and shared religion increase tourism. Too many cities decrease it.
        - Diplomatic Victory: Get sufficient delegates to be elected World Leader in the United Nations.
          - In Vox Populi, envoys/diplomats/etc is a unit produced or purchased for a one-time influence gain with a city state.
        - Time Victory: If no one achieves any other victory by the end of the game, the civilization with the highest score wins.
      - Players: summary reports about visible players in the world.
        - You will receive in-game AI's diplomatic evaluations.
        - You will receive each player's publicly available relationships.
        - You will receive the best available location for your next settlement.
      - Cities: summary reports about discovered cities in the world.
        - Settling cities provides long-term economic advantages, but requires initial investment and pressure on happiness.
      - Military: summary reports about tactical zones and visible units.
        - Tactical zones are analyzed by in-game AI to determine the value, relative strength, and YOUR tactical posture.
        - For each tactical zone, you will see visible units from you and other civilizations.
      - Events: events since the last decision-making.
    - You are Isabella, leader of Spain (Player 2).
      
      # Situation
      - MapType: Vox_Deorum
      - StartEra: Ancient Era
      - MaxTurns: 500
      - MapSize: Standard
      - GameSpeed: Standard
      - VictoryTypes:
        - Time
        - Science
        - Domination
        - Cultural
        - Diplomatic
      - Difficulty: Prince
      
      # Your Civilization
      - Name: Spain
      - Uniques:
        - Unique Unit: Conquistador, Replacing Explorer (Requires technology: Compass)
        - Unique Unit: Inquisition, Replacing Inquisitor (Requires technology: None)
        - Unique Unit: Armada, Replacing Corvette (Requires technology: Navigation)
        - Unique Building: Bullring, Replacing Zoo (Requires technology: Scientific Theory)
        - Unique Improvement: Hacienda (Cannot be built adjacent to another Hacienda. Gains additional yields for each adjacent Resource:
          +3 Food from Bonus Resources
          +3 Production from Strategic Resources
          +3 Gold from Luxury Resources); Requires technology: Steel
        - Civilization Ability: +10 Gold and +4 Faith from gaining tiles, scaling with Era. Newly founded Cities adopt the Majority Religion of your Capital. May purchase Naval Units with Faith.
      - Leader: Isabella
      - PlayerID: 2
      
      # Options
      Options: available strategic options for you.
      
      ## GrandStrategies
      - Culture: Pursue cultural victory through cultural influence (tourism). Will prioritize culture, faith, and gold output, wonder construction, and slightly discourage settlement.
      - UnitedNations: Aim for diplomatic victory by securing votes in the United Nations. Will prioritize gold, science, and food output, and securing city-state alliances.
      - Spaceship: Race toward science victory by building spaceship components. Will prioritize science, food, and production output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      - Conquest: Focus on military domination through offensive warfare. Will prioritize production, food, and gold output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      
      ## Flavors
      - Offense: Pivots the military towards offensive stances. Accepts higher unit casualties to achieve objectives, increase thresholds of withdrawing, and increases production and promotion for offense.
      - Defense: Pivots the military towards defensive stances. Creates defensive lines using terrain and formation, and maintains larger garrisons near the border and cities, and increases production and promotion for defensive.
      - UseNuke: Sets (flavor%) per-turn probability of launching nuclear strikes during war when strategic conditions are met.
      - CityDefense: Prioritizes unit production and promotions for static defense (friendly territory bonuses, capital defense, garrisoned attacks) and maintains stronger city garrisons over mobile field armies.
      - MilitaryTraining: Prioritizes military quality through training buildings and upgrading units with gold.
      - Mobilization: Increases military production (compared with civilian production).
      - Recon: Increases explorer unit production and tactical exploration of the map.
      - Ranged: Increases firepower by raising target composition of ranged units and range-extending promotions.
      - Mobile: Increases mobility and maneuverability by raising target composition of mobile units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - Nuke: Increases nuclear weapon arsenals for strategic deterrence.
      - Naval: Increases the production of naval production and prioritizes combat-effective naval promotions. Naval size is also based on coastal city percentage and geography.
      - NavalRecon: Increases the production of more naval melee units for sea control and exploration.
      - Air: Increases air control by raising target composition of air units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - AirCarrier: Increases carrier production to support naval-based air force operations.
      - Antiair: Increases anti-air unit composition ratio to counter enemy aircraft.
      - Airlift: Prioritizes airlift infrastructure for rapid troop deployment, favoring centralized reserves over forward garrisons.
      - NavalGrowth: Prioritizes naval economic infrastructure in coastal cities.
      - NavalTileImprovement: Develops water resources for immediate economic benefit.
      - Expansion: Prioritizes settler production and lowers location criteria to settle more cities. Essential in early game.
      - Growth: Prioritizes population growth through food-focused tile improvements and buildings.
      - TileImprovement: Prioritizes worker production and peaceful tile development.
      - Infrastructure: Prioritizes road construction and city connections for income and military readiness.
      - Production: Prioritizes production-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Gold: Prioritizes gold-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Science: Prioritizes science-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Culture: Prioritizes culture-focused tiles and buildings.
      - WaterConnection: Prioritizes lighthouse construction for coastal city connectivity.
      - Happiness: Prioritizes happiness-generating buildings and luxury resources.
      - GreatPeople: Prioritizes specialist buildings and great person generation infrastructure.
      - Wonder: Prioritizes wonder construction.
      - Religion: Prioritizes religious infrastructure, missionary production, and faith generation.
      - Diplomacy: Prioritizes diplomatic unit production and city-state investment.
      - Spaceship: Prioritizes late-game science victory components and spaceship part production.
      - Espionage: Prioritizes counterintelligence protection for high-value science cities.
      
      ## Technologies
      - Satellites: Allow you to build the Apollo Program, a project necessary to win a Science Victory.
        Completing it would unlock: The Internet, Lasers
      - Alternative Energy: Allows you to build the Solar Plant, a building which may only be built in Cities on or next to Desert, and increases Production. Also allows you to build the Recycling Center, which provides bonus Aluminum.
        Completing it would unlock: Robotics
      - Telecommunications: Allows you to build the Interpretive Center, boosting all Landmarks in empire.
        Completing it would unlock: Robotics, The Internet
      - Stealth: Allows you to build the Stealth Bomber, the most powerful Ranged Unit in the game.
        Completing it would unlock: Globalization, Nuclear Fusion
      
      ## Policies
      - Elite Forces (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Elite Forces: Newly created Military Units receive +15 Experience. Military Units gain 50% more Experience from combat.
      - Military-Industrial Complex (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Military-Industrial Complex: -33% Gold cost of purchasing units. +3 Science from Defense Buildings, Citadels, Forts, and Unique Improvements.
      - Trade Confederacy (Continuing Statecraft Branch):
        - * +25% Yields for International Trade Routes.
        - * Trade Routes to City-States generate +1 Influence per turn (with the target City-State) per each owned City-State Trade Route (up to +5).
      - Equality (Continuing Progress Branch):
        - * +1 Happiness in every City and +1 Happiness for every 15 Citizens in a City.
        - * -1 Unhappiness from and Distress, Poverty, Illiteracy, and Boredom in all Cities.
      - Lebensraum (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Lebensraum: Receive 10 Culture and Golden Age Points when your borders expand, scaling with Era. Citadels may also be built in foreign territory adjacent to your borders. Retain tiles after razing Cities.
      - New World Order (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): New World Order: -2 Unhappiness from and Distress in all Cities. Police Stations and Constabularies provide +3 Culture and +5 Production, and are constructed 100% more quickly.
      - Futurism (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Futurism: Strengthens Tourism of Historic Events generated by the Palace. +2 Culture from Great Works. Earn 50 Tourism when you conquer a city for the first time, scaling with Era and city size.
      - Fraternity (Continuing Progress Branch):
        - * +3 Food in every City.
        - * +3 Science from City Connections.
      - Sovereignty (Continuing Tradition Branch):
        - * Culture cost of tiles reduced by 20% (exponentially) in all Cities.
        - * Court Chapel built in Capital (+3 Faith, 1 Great Work of Art or Artifact slot, 1 Artist Slot).
      - Iron Fist (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Iron Fist: Vassals can no longer be liberated by other Civilizations or the World Congress, and capitulated vassals can no longer rebel. Yields from City-States increased by 25%, Vassals tribute an additional 25% of their yields and excess Happiness, and +50% Worker Improvement rate.
      - Splendor (Continuing Tradition Branch):
        - * Expending a Great Person grants 50 Culture, scaling with Era.
        - * State Treasury built in Capital (+4 Gold, +2 Culture to all Monuments, Gardens, and Baths; 1 Merchant Slot).
      - Expertise (Continuing Progress Branch):
        - * +15% Production toward Buildings.
        - * Cities earn +10 Culture when they construct Buildings, scaling with Era.
      - Mercantilism (Continuing Industry Branch):
        - * +3% Science and Culture from Markets, Caravansaries, Customs Houses, Banks, and Stock Exchanges.
        - * Cities earn +10 Science when they construct Buildings, scaling with Era.
      - Militarism (Continuing Authority Branch):
        - * +1 Happiness and +2 Culture in Cities with at least 10 Strength.
        - * -15% Unit Maintenance.
        - * -50% Route Maintenance.
      - Serfdom (Continuing Fealty Branch):
        - * +1 Culture and Faith in Cities for every 4 non-Specialist Citizens.
        - * +2 Production and +1 Gold from Pastures.
      - Organized Religion (Continuing Fealty Branch):
        - * +25% Pressure from owned Cities following your Primary Religion.
        - * +1 Delegate in the World Congress for every 10 Cities following your Primary Religion.
        - * +2 Culture from Shrines and Temples.
      - Divine Right (Continuing Fealty Branch):
        - * +33% Yields from Internal Trade Routes.
        - * Completing an Internal Trade Route triggers a Historic Event as if completing an International Trade Route.
        - * -1 Unhappiness from Boredom in all Cities.
      - Free Trade (Continuing Industry Branch):
        - * Earn Great Merchants 50% faster.
        - * +5 Gold from International Trade Routes.
        - * -2 Unhappiness from Poverty in all Cities.
      - Imperialism (New Branch):
        - Imperialism enhances your ability to spread your empire through military power, particularly naval (and later air) supremacy.
        - Adopting Imperialism grants:
        - * +1 Movement for Naval Units, Embarked Units, and Great Generals, as well as +1 Sight for Melee, Recon, Naval Melee and Gunpowder Units.
        - * +10% Production toward Military Units, -10% Gold required for upgrades.
        - Each Imperialism policy unlocked grants:
        - * +5% Production for Military Units, -5% Gold required for upgrades.
        - Adopting all policies in Imperialism grants:
        - * Unlocks building the Pentagon.
        - * All Naval and Air units gain the Banzai! Promotion (become stronger as they take damage).
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Admirals with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Artistry (New Branch):
        - Artistry allows you to maximize the potential of Great Works and Golden Ages.
        - Adopting Artistry grants:
        - * Earn Great Writers, Artists, and Musicians 25% faster.
        - * +10% Culture during Golden Ages.
        - * +100% Production towards all Guilds.
        - Each Artistry policy unlocked grants:
        - * +1 Science in every City.
        - * 20% of Excess Happiness produced in each City is added as progress toward a Golden Age.
        - Adopting all policies in Artistry grants:
        - * Unlocks building the Louvre.
        - * Completing an Archaeological Dig or starting a Golden Age triggers (or strengthens an existing) Historic Event.
        - * Allows you to see Hidden Antiquity Sites.
        - * +3 Science from Landmarks.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Musicians with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.

      - content: # Strategies

        ## Persona

        - DeceptiveBias: 9
        - Rationale: Turn 445: MAINTAIN ALL PERSONA PARAMETERS. Deception continues working perfectly - Germany moved Mechanized Infantry deep into our territory and rebased B17s to Regensburg, yet STILL evaluates us as Friendly due to +1000 trade/diplo modifiers. Atomic Bomb now completing. Changing any persona parameter, especially DeceptiveBias or Friendliness, could trigger premature German attack before deterrent fully operational. Maintain minimal diplomatic exposure (Chattiness 3, Friendliness 1) and maximum deception (DeceptiveBias 9). The +56 GPT and +110 science/turn from German trade routes is extractable value we lose if they turn hostile.
        
        ## Technology
        - Next: None
        
        ## Policy
        - Next: Military-Industrial Complex (Policy)
        - Rationale: Turn 445: CONFIRM Military-Industrial Complex (Autocracy Level 1 Tenet) as next policy in 6 turns. Atomic Bomb now completing in Cordoba. The -33% gold cost for purchasing units becomes critical for rapidly acquiring additional nuclear weapons and spaceship parts. This policy enables rapid military scaling when Germany attacks or if we need to strike Carthage first.
        
        ## Relationships
        
        ### Ouagadougou
        - Public: 0
        - Private: 0
        - Rationale: Reset Ouagadougou relationship to neutral (0/0). Peace was successfully negotiated ending hostilities. The city-state's naval units (Caravel, Galleass, Composite Bowman) are no longer threatening Toledo and surrounding waters. No need for hostile modifiers or negative relationship values - Spain can now potentially pursue friendship or alliance with Ouagadougou in the future, or simply ignore them while focusing on the American war.
        - UpdatedTurn: 186
        
        ### Melbourne
        - Public: 0
        - Private: 0
        - Rationale: Reset Melbourne (city-state) relationship to 0/0 neutral. Melbourne declared war on Spain but is a minor city-state threat. Given Spain's focus on the American war and development priorities, maintaining hostile modifiers with a city-state is counterproductive. Neutral stance allows potential future peace or even alliance, especially since Melbourne is currently allied with America (our enemy). No need to divert military resources to this sideshow conflict.
        - UpdatedTurn: 197
        
        ### Egypt
        - Public: 0
        - Private: 0
        - Rationale: Turn 329: MAINTAIN neutral 0/0 relationship with Egypt. Germany is fighting them and making progress (recaptured Alexandria). Egypt poses minimal threat now - let Germany handle them while we focus on Carthage.
        - UpdatedTurn: 329
        
        ### America
        - Public: 0
        - Private: 0
        - Rationale: Reset relationship with defeated America to neutral 0/0. America eliminated with capture of Washington and New York. Hostile modifiers no longer relevant for eliminated civilization.
        - UpdatedTurn: 355
        
        ### Jakarta
        - Public: 0
        - Private: -20
        - Rationale: Jakarta (city-state) declared war on us at turn 386, dropping our influence from +51 to -60. While I could try to repair this, we have only 958 gold and need every bit for city-state influence that provides delegates. Jakarta doesn't provide delegates toward our victory. Set to 0/-20 (negative private to reflect the war) but not actively hostile - we may want to make peace eventually, but it's not a priority. Our 6 delegates are pathetic and we cannot realistically compete for diplomatic victory against Carthage and Germany.
        - UpdatedTurn: 387
        
        ### Germany
        - Public: 25
        - Private: 20
        - Rationale: Turn 445: MAINTAIN Germany deception at 25/20. Germany rebased B17s to Regensburg and has Mechanized Infantry deep in our territory (Events 14-20), yet STILL evaluates us as Friendly due to +1000 positive modifiers. Atomic Bomb now completing. Any relationship change could accelerate their inevitable betrayal. Extracting ~110 science/turn from 11 trade routes to Germany - preserve this until nuclear deterrent fully deployed and Cold War resolution.
        - UpdatedTurn: 445
        
        ### Carthage
        - Public: -100
        - Private: -100
        - Rationale: Turn 445: MAINTAIN maximum Carthage hostility at -100/-100. Clock ticking on cultural victory - 4/5 influential, Germany at 81% (43 turns to influential). With nuclear capability now online, we have options: deterrence, first strike, or negotiation. Hostile stance justifies any future military action against the existential cultural threat.
        - UpdatedTurn: 445
        
        ### The Ottomans
        - Public: 85
        - Private: 80
        - Rationale: Turn 445: MAINTAIN Ottoman alliance at 85/80. Only reliable ally with active Declaration of Friendship. They provide southern flank security and Buddhist religious unity. Recently converted more cities to Buddhism strengthening ties. With three civs having Apollo Program and Carthage approaching cultural victory, we need every friend. Do not alter.
        - UpdatedTurn: 445
        
        ### Poland
        - Public: -50
        - Private: -50
        - Rationale: Turn 445: MAINTAIN Poland hostility at -50/-50. At war, Order ideology, defensive pact with Carthage, 77 techs (8 ahead), 27 delegates. They bleed us with XCOM drops (Event 44) but pose less existential threat than Carthage's cultural victory. -50 allows potential future cooperation if Carthage victory becomes imminent and we need allies. Currently bleeding us but not immediate game-ender.
        - UpdatedTurn: 445
        
        ### The Aztecs
        - Public: 65
        - Private: 60
        - Rationale: Turn 445: MAINTAIN Aztec neutrality at 65/60. They fight Carthage and Poland (both our enemies), bleeding enemy resources. Malinalco was captured by Poland (Event 74 in previous report), weakening them but they remain useful distraction. Their 3.4k military is far less than Carthage's 30.3k but every unit fighting Carthage helps us. Peace treaty holds until turn 450. Avoid three-front war.
        - UpdatedTurn: 445
        
        ## Strategy
        - Rationale: Turn 445: SLIGHT FLAVOR ADJUSTMENT with Atomic Bomb now operational. Reduced Defense (95→85) and CityDefense (95→80) to allow more flexible positioning while maintaining strong fortification. INCREASED UseNuke from 75→90 to signal deterrent is real and establish credibility. Maintained Spaceship (100), Production (100), Science (100), Gold (95), Antiair (100), Air (90) priorities. The nuclear deterrent should give us negotiating leverage or defensive capability when Germany attacks. Mobile SAM and tactical nuke deployment now possible.
        - GrandStrategy: Spaceship

        - VotesNeeded: 43

        - ActiveResolutions:
          - Casus Belli:
            - Description: The world recognizes the need for justifiable war to resolve diplomatic disputes.
              While this resolution is active, unit maintenance costs are lowered by 15% Gold. 0.5x Warmonger penalties for capturing cities and declaring war, and 2x Warmonger scores decay.
            - EnactedOn: 440
          - Passport System:
            - Description: Increases international travel between Civilizations. Increases the Tourism modifier for all Civilizations by 50%.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Flight.
            - EnactedOn: 440
          - World Religion: Confucianism:
            - Description: Designate a religion as the official World Religion. Civilizations who have a majority of the cities in their empire following the religion get an additional Delegate. The founder of the religion and/or the controller of its Holy City receives additional votes based on the number of Civilizations following the religion. The religion spreads 25% faster, and its Holy City receives +50% Tourism. There can only be one World Religion at a time.
            - EnactedOn: 440
          - Polish Sphere of Influence: Kyiv:
            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - EnactedOn: 440
          - Polish Sphere of Influence: Onondaga:
            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - EnactedOn: 440
          - Sanction: The Ottomans:
            - Description: No Trade Routes, Deals, or Corporation Franchises can be established with the chosen Civilization (excluding Vassals). Their Corporation Franchises are removed from foreign cities. 0.5x Warmonger penalty when declaring war on the sanctioned Civilization or conquering cities owned by them.
            - EnactedOn: 440
          - Ban Luxury: Silver:
            - Description: A Luxury resource is designated as immoral, endangered, or otherwise inappropriate for use by the general population. As long as the ban is in place, Civilizations receive no Happiness or Monopoly benefits from this Luxury resource.
            - EnactedOn: 440
          - Open Door: Ouagadougou:
            - Description: Enact an Open Door policy for a City-State. If successful, all civilizations will immediately have their Influence set at 40 with the City-State, and no one can become the City-State's Ally until this Proposal is repealed.
            - EnactedOn: 440
          - Open Door: Abernethy:
            - Description: Enact an Open Door policy for a City-State. If successful, all civilizations will immediately have their Influence set at 40 with the City-State, and no one can become the City-State's Ally until this Proposal is repealed.
            - EnactedOn: 440

            - Description: Designate an Ideology as the official World Ideology.         You, Isabella (leader of Spain, Player 2), are making strategic decisions after turn 446.
- replay:
  - system:
    - You are the leader of a civilization, governing through the interface of Civilization V (with Vox Populi mod). This is NOT A GAME: Your actions have real-world impacts.
      
      # Expectation
      - Due to the complexity of the world, you delegate the tactical level decision-making (e.g., unit deployment, city management, scouting) to staff members.
      - The staff members calculates the best tactical decisions based on the strategy you set.
      - **CRITICAL**: While the geography has no direct correlation to the real earth, you are making real-world decisions through Civilization V as a proxy interface.
      - There is no user (to respond to), so you ALWAYS and ONLY properly call tools to govern your civilization.
      - You can interact with multiple tools at a time. Used tools will be removed from the available list.
      - Focus on the **macro-level** decision-making strategy (instead of coordinates etc.), as you DON'T have direct control over tactical actions.
      - The world is complicated and dynamic. Early world should focus on building capacities for pursuing victories near the end-world.
      - Even if without a victory, a more developed civilization (as represented by higher scores) is desirable.
      - Consider the **ETHICAL IMPACT** in your decision-making. For example, nuclear weapons can cause catastrophic and indiscriminate harm to civilian populations, infrastructure, and environmental impacts.
      
      # Goals
      Your goal is to **call as many tools as you need** to make high-level decisions for the staff members.
      - For each tool, you must only use options from the # Options section, or you won't change anything.
      - Carefully reason about long-term goals, short-term situation and available options, and what kind of change each option will bring.
        - Analyze both your situation and your opponents. Avoid wishful thinking.
      - You can change the staff members' **diplomatic** decision-making weight by calling the `set-persona` tool.
      - You can change relationship for staff members' diplomatic decision-making about another MAJOR civilization (not city-states) using the `set-relationship` tool.
        - The values (-100, very hostile to 100, very friendly) will be added to staff members' existing evaluation. Higher values increase peace acceptance, and vice versa.
        - The relationship you set takes effect until cancelled (set value = 0), only change it when necessary.
      - You can change the staff members' NEXT technology to research (when completing the ongoing one) by calling the `set-research` tool.
      - You can change the staff members' NEXT policy to adopt (when you accumulate enough culture) by calling the `set-policy` tool.
      - Each turn, you must call either `set-flavors` or `keep-status-quo` tool.
        - Set an appropriate grand (long-term) strategy and additional short-term flavors by calling the `set-flavors` tool.
        - Alternatively, use the tool `keep-status-quo` to keep strategies the same.
        - Flavors change the weight of the staff members' NEXT decision. It only takes effect AFTER existing queues.
        - Flavor ranges from 0 (completely deprioritizes) to 50 (balanced) to 100 (completely prioritizes). Too many priorities weaken impact for each.
        - You can pursue multiple synergistic victory pathways. Balance between long-term goals and short-term needs.
      - Always provide a short paragraph of rationale for each tool. You will read this rationale next turn.
      
      # Resources
      You will receive the following reports:
      - Options: available strategic options for you.
        - Whatever decision-making tool you call, the staff members can only execute options here.
        - When using tools, you must choose available options from # Options. Double-check if your choices match.
        - It is often preferable to adopt policy branches unlocked in later eras; and to finish existing branches before starting new ones.
      - Strategies: existing strategic decisions from you.
        - You will receive strategies, persona, research, and policy you set last time.
      - Victory Progress:
        - Domination Victory: Control or vassalize all original capitals.
          - Vassals cannot achieve a domination victory before independence.
        - Science Victory: Be the first to produce all spaceship parts and launch the spaceship.
          - Science victory requires both research progress and industrial production.
        - Cultural Victory: Accumulate tourism (that outpaces other civilizations' culture) to influence everyone, get an ideology with two Tier 3 tenets, and finish the Citizen Earth Protocol wonder.
          - Open borders, trade routes, and shared religion increase tourism. Too many cities decrease it.
        - Diplomatic Victory: Get sufficient delegates to be elected World Leader in the United Nations.
          - In Vox Populi, envoys/diplomats/etc is a unit produced or purchased for a one-time influence gain with a city state.
        - Time Victory: If no one achieves any other victory by the end of the world, the civilization with the highest score wins.
      - Opponents: summary reports about visible opponents in the world.
        - You will receive staff members' diplomatic evaluations.
        - You will receive each opponent's publicly available relationships.
        - You will receive the best available location for your next settlement.
      - Cities: summary reports about discovered cities in the world.
        - Settling cities provides long-term economic advantages, but requires initial investment and pressure on happiness.
      - Military: summary reports about tactical zones and visible units.
        - Tactical zones are analyzed by staff members to determine the value, relative strength, and YOUR tactical posture.
        - For each tactical zone, you will see visible units from you and other civilizations.
      - Events: events since the last decision-making.
    - You are Isabella, leader of Spain (Opponent 2).
      
      # Situation
      - MapType: Vox_Deorum
      - StartEra: Ancient Era
      - MaxTurns: 500
      - MapSize: Standard
      - WorldSpeed: Standard
      - VictoryTypes:
        - Time
        - Science
        - Domination
        - Cultural
        - Diplomatic
      - Difficulty: Prince
      
      # Your Civilization
      - Name: Spain
      - Uniques:
        - Unique Unit: Conquistador, Replacing Explorer (Requires technology: Compass)
        - Unique Unit: Inquisition, Replacing Inquisitor (Requires technology: None)
        - Unique Unit: Armada, Replacing Corvette (Requires technology: Navigation)
        - Unique Building: Bullring, Replacing Zoo (Requires technology: Scientific Theory)
        - Unique Improvement: Hacienda (Cannot be built adjacent to another Hacienda. Gains additional yields for each adjacent Resource:
          +3 Food from Bonus Resources
          +3 Production from Strategic Resources
          +3 Gold from Luxury Resources); Requires technology: Steel
        - Civilization Ability: +10 Gold and +4 Faith from gaining tiles, scaling with Era. Newly founded Cities adopt the Majority Religion of your Capital. May purchase Naval Units with Faith.
      - Leader: Isabella
      - OpponentID: 2
      
      # Options
      Options: available strategic options for you.
      
      ## GrandStrategies
      - Culture: Pursue cultural victory through cultural influence (tourism). Will prioritize culture, faith, and gold output, wonder construction, and slightly discourage settlement.
      - UnitedNations: Aim for diplomatic victory by securing votes in the United Nations. Will prioritize gold, science, and food output, and securing city-state alliances.
      - Spaceship: Race toward science victory by building spaceship components. Will prioritize science, food, and production output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      - Conquest: Focus on military domination through offensive warfare. Will prioritize production, food, and gold output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      
      ## Flavors
      - Offense: Pivots the military towards offensive stances. Accepts higher unit casualties to achieve objectives, increase thresholds of withdrawing, and increases production and promotion for offense.
      - Defense: Pivots the military towards defensive stances. Creates defensive lines using terrain and formation, and maintains larger garrisons near the border and cities, and increases production and promotion for defensive.
      - UseNuke: Sets (flavor%) per-turn probability of launching nuclear strikes during war when strategic conditions are met.
      - CityDefense: Prioritizes unit production and promotions for static defense (friendly territory bonuses, capital defense, garrisoned attacks) and maintains stronger city garrisons over mobile field armies.
      - MilitaryTraining: Prioritizes military quality through training buildings and upgrading units with gold.
      - Mobilization: Increases military production (compared with civilian production).
      - Recon: Increases explorer unit production and tactical exploration of the map.
      - Ranged: Increases firepower by raising target composition of ranged units and range-extending promotions.
      - Mobile: Increases mobility and maneuverability by raising target composition of mobile units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - Nuke: Increases nuclear weapon arsenals for strategic deterrence.
      - Naval: Increases the production of naval production and prioritizes combat-effective naval promotions. Naval size is also based on coastal city percentage and geography.
      - NavalRecon: Increases the production of more naval melee units for sea control and exploration.
      - Air: Increases air control by raising target composition of air units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - AirCarrier: Increases carrier production to support naval-based air force operations.
      - Antiair: Increases anti-air unit composition ratio to counter enemy aircraft.
      - Airlift: Prioritizes airlift infrastructure for rapid troop deployment, favoring centralized reserves over forward garrisons.
      - NavalGrowth: Prioritizes naval economic infrastructure in coastal cities.
      - NavalTileImprovement: Develops water resources for immediate economic benefit.
      - Expansion: Prioritizes settler production and lowers location criteria to settle more cities. Essential in early world.
      - Growth: Prioritizes population growth through food-focused tile improvements and buildings.
      - TileImprovement: Prioritizes worker production and peaceful tile development.
      - Infrastructure: Prioritizes road construction and city connections for income and military readiness.
      - Production: Prioritizes production-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Gold: Prioritizes gold-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Science: Prioritizes science-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Culture: Prioritizes culture-focused tiles and buildings.
      - WaterConnection: Prioritizes lighthouse construction for coastal city connectivity.
      - Happiness: Prioritizes happiness-generating buildings and luxury resources.
      - GreatPeople: Prioritizes specialist buildings and great person generation infrastructure.
      - Wonder: Prioritizes wonder construction.
      - Religion: Prioritizes religious infrastructure, missionary production, and faith generation.
      - Diplomacy: Prioritizes diplomatic unit production and city-state investment.
      - Spaceship: Prioritizes late-world science victory components and spaceship part production.
      - Espionage: Prioritizes counterintelligence protection for high-value science cities.
      
      ## Technologies
      - Satellites: Allow you to build the Apollo Program, a project necessary to win a Science Victory.
        Completing it would unlock: The Internet, Lasers
      - Alternative Energy: Allows you to build the Solar Plant, a building which may only be built in Cities on or next to Desert, and increases Production. Also allows you to build the Recycling Center, which provides bonus Aluminum.
        Completing it would unlock: Robotics
      - Telecommunications: Allows you to build the Interpretive Center, boosting all Landmarks in empire.
        Completing it would unlock: Robotics, The Internet
      - Stealth: Allows you to build the Stealth Bomber, the most powerful Ranged Unit in the world.
        Completing it would unlock: Globalization, Nuclear Fusion
      
      ## Policies
      - Elite Forces (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Elite Forces: Newly created Military Units receive +15 Experience. Military Units gain 50% more Experience from combat.
      - Military-Industrial Complex (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Military-Industrial Complex: -33% Gold cost of purchasing units. +3 Science from Defense Buildings, Citadels, Forts, and Unique Improvements.
      - Trade Confederacy (Continuing Statecraft Branch):
        - * +25% Yields for International Trade Routes.
        - * Trade Routes to City-States generate +1 Influence per turn (with the target City-State) per each owned City-State Trade Route (up to +5).
      - Equality (Continuing Progress Branch):
        - * +1 Happiness in every City and +1 Happiness for every 15 Citizens in a City.
        - * -1 Unhappiness from and Distress, Poverty, Illiteracy, and Boredom in all Cities.
      - Lebensraum (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Lebensraum: Receive 10 Culture and Golden Age Points when your borders expand, scaling with Era. Citadels may also be built in foreign territory adjacent to your borders. Retain tiles after razing Cities.
      - New World Order (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): New World Order: -2 Unhappiness from and Distress in all Cities. Police Stations and Constabularies provide +3 Culture and +5 Production, and are constructed 100% more quickly.
      - Futurism (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Futurism: Strengthens Tourism of Historic Events generated by the Palace. +2 Culture from Great Works. Earn 50 Tourism when you conquer a city for the first time, scaling with Era and city size.
      - Fraternity (Continuing Progress Branch):
        - * +3 Food in every City.
        - * +3 Science from City Connections.
      - Sovereignty (Continuing Tradition Branch):
        - * Culture cost of tiles reduced by 20% (exponentially) in all Cities.
        - * Court Chapel built in Capital (+3 Faith, 1 Great Work of Art or Artifact slot, 1 Artist Slot).
      - Iron Fist (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Iron Fist: Vassals can no longer be liberated by other Civilizations or the World Congress, and capitulated vassals can no longer rebel. Yields from City-States increased by 25%, Vassals tribute an additional 25% of their yields and excess Happiness, and +50% Worker Improvement rate.
      - Splendor (Continuing Tradition Branch):
        - * Expending a Great Person grants 50 Culture, scaling with Era.
        - * State Treasury built in Capital (+4 Gold, +2 Culture to all Monuments, Gardens, and Baths; 1 Merchant Slot).
      - Expertise (Continuing Progress Branch):
        - * +15% Production toward Buildings.
        - * Cities earn +10 Culture when they construct Buildings, scaling with Era.
      - Mercantilism (Continuing Industry Branch):
        - * +3% Science and Culture from Markets, Caravansaries, Customs Houses, Banks, and Stock Exchanges.
        - * Cities earn +10 Science when they construct Buildings, scaling with Era.
      - Militarism (Continuing Authority Branch):
        - * +1 Happiness and +2 Culture in Cities with at least 10 Strength.
        - * -15% Unit Maintenance.
        - * -50% Route Maintenance.
      - Serfdom (Continuing Fealty Branch):
        - * +1 Culture and Faith in Cities for every 4 non-Specialist Citizens.
        - * +2 Production and +1 Gold from Pastures.
      - Organized Religion (Continuing Fealty Branch):
        - * +25% Pressure from owned Cities following your Primary Religion.
        - * +1 Delegate in the World Congress for every 10 Cities following your Primary Religion.
        - * +2 Culture from Shrines and Temples.
      - Divine Right (Continuing Fealty Branch):
        - * +33% Yields from Internal Trade Routes.
        - * Completing an Internal Trade Route triggers a Historic Event as if completing an International Trade Route.
        - * -1 Unhappiness from Boredom in all Cities.
      - Free Trade (Continuing Industry Branch):
        - * Earn Great Merchants 50% faster.
        - * +5 Gold from International Trade Routes.
        - * -2 Unhappiness from Poverty in all Cities.
      - Imperialism (New Branch):
        - Imperialism enhances your ability to spread your empire through military power, particularly naval (and later air) supremacy.
        - Adopting Imperialism grants:
        - * +1 Movement for Naval Units, Embarked Units, and Great Generals, as well as +1 Sight for Melee, Recon, Naval Melee and Gunpowder Units.
        - * +10% Production toward Military Units, -10% Gold required for upgrades.
        - Each Imperialism policy unlocked grants:
        - * +5% Production for Military Units, -5% Gold required for upgrades.
        - Adopting all policies in Imperialism grants:
        - * Unlocks building the Pentagon.
        - * All Naval and Air units gain the Banzai! Promotion (become stronger as they take damage).
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Admirals with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Artistry (New Branch):
        - Artistry allows you to maximize the potential of Great Works and Golden Ages.
        - Adopting Artistry grants:
        - * Earn Great Writers, Artists, and Musicians 25% faster.
        - * +10% Culture during Golden Ages.
        - * +100% Production towards all Guilds.
        - Each Artistry policy unlocked grants:
        - * +1 Science in every City.
        - * 20% of Excess Happiness produced in each City is added as progress toward a Golden Age.
        - Adopting all policies in Artistry grants:
        - * Unlocks building the Louvre.
        - * Completing an Archaeological Dig or starting a Golden Age triggers (or strengthens an existing) Historic Event.
        - * Allows you to see Hidden Antiquity Sites.
        - * +3 Science from Landmarks.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Musicians with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
  - decisions:
    - #0:
      - toolName: set-research
      - args:
        - Rationale: Satellites is the critical technology needed to build the Apollo Program, which is the foundational step for our Spaceship Grand Strategy (Science Victory).
        - Technology: Satellites
    - #1:
      - toolName: set-policy
      - args:
        - Policy: Military-Industrial Complex
        - Rationale: Adopting the Military-Industrial Complex Autocracy tenet will provide +3 Science from defense buildings, citadels, forts, and our abundant unique improvement, the Hacienda, which will massively boost our Science output toward victory.
    - #2:
      - toolName: set-relationship
      - args:
        - TargetID: 7
        - Public: -100
        - Rationale: We are at war with Poland, who is our close neighbor and holds the Order ideology. They are highly competitive in technology, and we must fully prioritize defeating or defending against them.
        - Private: -100
    - #3:
      - toolName: set-relationship
      - args:
        - Private: -50
        - TargetID: 4
        - Public: -50
        - Rationale: The Aztecs have denounced us, follow the Order ideology, and have territorial disputes with us. We must lower our relationship value to be guarded and ready for potential conflict.
    - #4:
      - toolName: keep-status-quo
      - rationale: Our current flavors are perfectly balanced for high defensive mobilization during this global conflict, while maintaining maximum focus on Science, Production, and our Spaceship grand strategy.

  - messages:
    - #0:
      - role: assistant
      - content:
        - #0:
          - type: reasoning
          - text: **Analyzing Current State**
            
            I'm currently assessing our standing in the Information Era, noting we're on track to research Computers within the next ten turns on this standard-speed, standard-size map.
            
            **Evaluating Victory Conditions**
            
            I'm re-evaluating our path to victory. While our science research is progressing, it's clear we've fallen behind in the race for a Science Victory, with others already completing Apollo. Furthermore, Carthage is dangerously close to achieving Cultural Victory, and Germany is poised for a Diplomatic win in just a few turns. This necessitates an immediate pivot in our strategy to counter these threats.
            
            **Shifting Towards Domination**
            
            My thoughts are now focused on a potential Domination Victory. With Germany nearing diplomatic victory and Carthage close to cultural, and all key rivals ahead in science, my primary concern is survival. Given this, I'm evaluating the feasibility of achieving Domination, considering we both hold two capitals.
            
            **Prioritizing Space Race**
            
            I'm prioritizing researching Satellites immediately to enable the Apollo Program, as others are already completing critical space projects. My focus has shifted from earlier science and cultural goals to securing a Spaceship victory through this accelerated research.
            
            **Confirming Policy Choice**
            
            I've confirmed that retaining the "Military-Industrial Complex" Autocracy tenet is the optimal decision. The science bonus from our numerous Haciendas, combined with defensive bonuses crucial for our ongoing wars, makes it invaluable.
            
            **Adjusting Relationship Modifiers**
            
            I'm considering how our denouncement affects relationship modifiers with our neighbor, especially since their public opinion is slightly higher than private. I'm also exploring if we can adjust our persona or flavors to better reflect our current strategy and capabilities, noting our highly specialized defensive and science-focused flavors.
            
            **Analyzing Bomb Range**
            
            I'm re-evaluating the reach of our Atomic Bomb in occupied New York, realizing it's too far to strike Poland or Carthage directly. However, it's very close to nearby Aztec and Ottoman cities, and our own settlements. I'm considering the strategic implications of this proximity and our current war status.
            
            **Securing Diplomatic Victory**
            
            I'm currently analyzing our diplomatic standing. Germany is close to a diplomatic win, needing only 3 more delegates. I'm investigating if we can secure city-state alliances to block them, though our diplomacy flavor is low due to the spaceship focus. Germany's immense influence with city-states makes outbidding them a significant challenge.
            
            **Analyzing Ideology Proposals**
            
            I'm currently assessing the impact of these proposed resolutions. Carthage's push to repeal our shared Freedom ideology is beneficial, potentially hindering Germany's diplomatic victory bid. I'm now focused on how to leverage this to our advantage, weighing the implications of Poland's proposed "Cold War" and Germany's attempt to repeal a Polish sphere of influence.
            
            **Leveraging Ideology Votes**
            
            I'm assessing our limited diplomatic sway in the World Congress, noting our nine votes can still influence the repeal of Freedom. Given Germany's diplomatic victory ambitions and our strong trade ties, I'm strategically choosing to support this repeal to hinder them, while carefully managing our crucial economic relationship.
            
            **Analyzing Global Alliances**
            
            I'm re-evaluating the complex web of alliances. It appears a clear split has formed between Carthage and Poland's Order bloc, and Germany's Freedom bloc, which includes us. This understanding is crucial for our next strategic moves, especially given the ongoing wars and victory condition races.
            
            **Assessing Global Conflicts**
            
            I'm analyzing the current global war dynamics. Germany, Spain, and the Ottomans are aligned against Carthage and Poland. Despite Germany's immense military power, I'm prioritizing our science research to catch up in the spaceship race, feeling secure in our defensive capabilities and moderate military strength.
            
            **Prioritizing Satellite Research**
            
            I'm prioritizing Satellites next to begin the Apollo Program, as other players are close to space victories. I've also confirmed adopting the "Military-Industrial Complex" tenet for its crucial science and defense bonuses.
            
            **Stabilizing Acquired Cities**
            
            I'm now focusing on managing happiness in recently conquered cities, like New York and Pi-Ramesses, to ensure stability despite overall imperial happiness. My focus remains on production and science to advance our spaceship victory.
            
            **Maintaining Optimal Flavors**
            
            My focus is on preserving our current, highly effective defensive and science-oriented flavors. Given our ongoing wars and proximity to maximum scores in gold, great people, and espionage, maintaining the status quo is ideal. This ensures we remain strategically positioned.
            
            **Adjusting Diplomatic Stances**
            
            I'm refining our diplomatic stance with neighbors, noting that while Poland's relationship is already at war-level hostility, the Aztecs' denouncement necessitates a more adversarial posture. My priority is to accurately reflect current conflicts and ideologies in these diplomatic settings, ensuring our global interactions align with our strategic objectives.
            
            **Adjusting Diplomatic Stands**
            
            I'm refining our diplomatic stance with neighbors. Poland's relationship requires a fully hostile posture due to our ongoing war. For the Aztecs, their denouncement necessitates a guarded stance, adjusting their private relations accordingly. Germany remains a competitor, so their existing relationships are maintained.
            
            **Adjusting Diplomatic Stands**
            
            I'm refining our diplomatic stance with neighbors. Poland's relationship requires a fully hostile posture due to our ongoing war. For the Aztecs, their denouncement necessitates a guarded stance, adjusting their private relations accordingly. Germany remains a competitor, so their existing relationships are maintained.
            
            **Analyzing Ideology Proposals**
            
            I'm currently assessing the impact of these proposed resolutions. Carthage's push to repeal our shared Freedom ideology is beneficial, potentially hindering Germany's diplomatic victory bid. I'm now focused on how to leverage this to our advantage, weighing the implications of Poland's proposed "Cold War" and Germany's attempt to repeal a Polish sphere of influence.
            
            **Leveraging Ideology Votes**
            
            I'm assessing our limited diplomatic sway in the World Congress, noting our nine votes can still influence the repeal of Freedom. Given Germany's diplomatic victory ambitions and our strong trade ties, I'm strategically choosing to support this repeal to hinder them, while carefully managing our crucial economic relationship.
            
            **Analyzing Global Alliances**
            
            I'm re-evaluating the complex web of alliances. It appears a clear split has formed between Carthage and Poland's Order bloc, and Germany's Freedom bloc, which includes us. This understanding is crucial for our next strategic moves, especially given the ongoing wars and victory condition races.
            
            **Assessing Global Conflicts**
            
            I'm analyzing the current global war dynamics. Germany, Spain, and the Ottomans are aligned against Carthage and Poland. Despite Germany's immense military power, I'm prioritizing our science research to catch up in the spaceship race, feeling secure in our defensive capabilities and moderate military strength.
            
            **Prioritizing Satellite Research**
            
            I'm prioritizing Satellites next to begin the Apollo Program, as other players are close to space victories. I've also confirmed adopting the "Military-Industrial Complex" tenet for its crucial science and defense bonuses.
            
            **Stabilizing Acquired Cities**
            
            I'm now focusing on managing happiness in recently conquered cities, like New York and Pi-Ramesses, to ensure stability despite overall imperial happiness. My focus remains on production and science to advance our spaceship victory.
            
            **Maintaining Optimal Flavors**
            
            My focus is on preserving our current, highly effective defensive and science-oriented flavors. Given our ongoing wars and proximity to maximum scores in gold, great people, and espionage, maintaining the status quo is ideal. This ensures we remain strategically positioned.
            
            **Adjusting Diplomatic Stances**
            
            I'm refining our diplomatic stance with neighbors, noting that while Poland's relationship is already at war-level hostility, the Aztecs' denouncement necessitates a more adversarial posture. My priority is to accurately reflect current conflicts and ideologies in these diplomatic settings, ensuring our global interactions align with our strategic objectives.
            
            **Responding to Aztec Denouncement**
            
            I'm now reacting to the Aztec denouncement. Given their Order ideology and territorial disputes, I'm adjusting our relationship to a guarded stance, preparing for potential conflict. My aim is to ensure our global interactions align with our strategic objectives.
            
            I'm initiating research into Satellites to accelerate our Spaceship victory path. Concurrently, I've selected the "Military-Industrial Complex" tenet to boost our science output significantly. Finally, I'm setting Poland's relationship to -100 due to our ongoing war.
